- **spgr-agent-architect**: Produces two or more genuinely distinct architecture options for human selection, then writes the full downstream artifact set (ADRs, ERD, API spec, diagrams, tech stack, data dictionary) for the selected option. - **spgr-agent-async-infrastructure**: Owns the patterns and standards for background jobs, webhook delivery, and transactional email. - **spgr-agent-auth**: The authority on authentication, authorization, sessions, and identity. - **spk-cr-correctness**: Specialist reviewer in the spk-code-review pipeline. ([sparkgeo/sparkgeo-ai](https://github.com/sparkgeo/sparkgeo-ai/blob/HEAD/plugins/spk-code-review/agents/spk-cr-correctness.md))
- **spk-cr-dependency**: Conditional specialist in the spk-code-review pipeline. Runs when package manifests, lockfiles, container base images, or build dependencies change. - **spk-cr-docs**: Conditional specialist in the spk-code-review pipeline. - **spk-cr-infrastructure**: Conditional specialist in the spk-code-review pipeline. - **spk-cr-maintainability**: Specialist reviewer in the spk-code-review pipeline.
